Let a = shift_A100_sint32(graph_0, 0).
Let x = 100 * n.
Let a_1 = shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, 0).
Assume {
  Type: is_sint32(i) /\ is_sint32(n).
  (* Heap *)
  Type: (region(dist_0.base) <= 0) /\ (region(graph_0.base) <= 0) /\
      linked(Malloc_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: (0 < n) /\ (n <= 100).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: valid_rd(Malloc_0, a, x).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < n) ->
      valid_rd(Malloc_0, shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(graph_0, i_1), 0), n))).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: valid_rw(Malloc_0, a_1, x).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < n) ->
      valid_rw(Malloc_0, shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, i_1), 0), n))).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: separated(a, x, a_1, x).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_matrix_bounded(Mint_1, graph_0, n).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_diagonal_zero(Mint_1, graph_0, n).
  (* Loop assigns ... *)
  Have: forall a_2 : addr.
      ((forall i_2,i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < n) -> ((0 <= i_1) ->
        ((i_1 < n) ->
        (shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, i_2), i_1) != a_2)))))) ->
      (Mint_0[a_2] = Mint_1[a_2])).
  (* Invariant 'i_0' *)
  Have: (0 <= i) /\ (i <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_1' *)
  Have: forall i_2,i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < i) -> ((0 <= i_1) ->
      ((i_1 < n) ->
      (Mint_0[shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(graph_0, i_2), i_1)]
         = Mint_0[shift_sint32(shift_A100_sint32(dist_0, i_2), i_1)]))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n <= i.
}
Prove: P_matrix_bounded(Mint_0, dist_0, i).
